### LB1a fixes done (review of 19940e45: a device never loses sight of its own label, same-name labels join, every writer keeps hidden labels and tags)
- **1 own label hidden from itself:** after a merge, a label (or tag scope) whose list this device was in and whose merged list lacks it is written again as merged + this device, stamped just after the winner (`TaskScopes.RejoinedLabels/RejoinedScopes`, called by `TaskListRules.MergeLists` and `MergeFile`; Kotlin `rejoinedLabels/rejoinedScopes` in `TaskListMerge.mergeLists` / `TaskListRules.mergeFile`). Fixtures `merge-lists-rejoin` (12) and `converge` (4 orders, A and B offline + a third device C: all end with OnlyOn {A,B}, each sees it, lists and scopes byte-equal). The old "newest list wins whole" check is now a merge-level check only. ADR 89 text rewritten (join rule; the "accepted" paragraph is gone; the constraint recorded), `hard-rules.md` too.
- **2 same name joins, never shared:** `TaskLabelEdits.Add` (C#) / `addWithId` (Kotlin) always join a hidden same-name label; phone `ShelfController.createLabel` and `TaskCategoriesStore.add` go through it; `recolour/rename` by name (Kotlin, phone categories) join first (`self`/`removed` params), `delete` of a hidden label does nothing. "Make shared" stays a separate action (LB1b/c UI).
- **3 every writer keeps hidden:** new `TaskScopes.KeepHidden(before, after, self, removed, now)` / `keepHidden` (keeps hidden tags and label; a hidden tag or label the user set is joined). Wired: PC `TaskActions.SetLabel/AddTags/RemoveTags/ToggleTag`, `TaskListRules.Place`, `ApplyLocal` (all take `removed`, required), `TaskStore` restore; `TaskCategoryEdits.Apply` takes `removed` (required) and computes the hidden set itself (no `hiddenLabels`). Phone: `ShelfEdits.each` (edit, editAll, dropOnPage), `ShelfEdits.add`, `TaskLocalEdits.apply`, restore. Keep both and Do again copy the whole task (label and tags included), so nothing hidden is lost there. Migration runs only before any scope exists. Per-writer fixtures and mutations (below).
- **4 name lookups join:** `TaskCategoryEdits.Apply` (visible by id, then visible by name, last a hidden one: joined); `ApplyLocal`/`TaskLocalEdits.apply` through `KeepHidden`; typed tags through `KeepHidden`; `TaskLabelBook.IdForName` (old fields read from ANOTHER device) deliberately still binds by name: that device's older app shows the label, the task keeps its id.
- **5 rename sees hidden labels:** `CheckName`/`checkName` ignore hidden labels only when `labelId` is null.
- **6 restore:** `TaskListRules.RestoredIncoming` / `restoredIncoming` (PC `TaskStore.ImportAsync(backup, restore, backupDevice)`, phone `PhoneTaskStore.import`): backup device swapped for this one in label lists and tag scopes, `OnlyOnStamp` re-stamped now like scopes, hidden labels/tags the task holds are kept. The PC backup (`TaskListsExport.Device`) and the phone's task backup (`device`) now carry the writing device; an older backup has none (no swap).
- **NITs:** unreadable `tasks.tagScopes` is copied to `tasks.tagScopes.unreadable` (first kept) before the next save; `SetOnlyOn`/`setOnlyOn` stamp the list only (`WithOnlyOn`), never the label's `EditedAt`; `tagScopes` capped at 2000 (`TagScopeMerge.MaxScopes`: oldest tombstones first, then oldest live), checks in C# and Kotlin; m3/m8 redone null-safe. **Known flaw kept (same as `ringOn`):** an older app can revive a deleted label without the list stamp, so a device holding the old live list can hand it back through the whole-label merge; only visibility.
- **For LB1b (PC) and LB1c (phone):** (1) **never offer "remove device X"**; a scope change on a label several devices share uses `Joined` (C# `TaskLabelEdits.Joined`, Kotlin `joined`), `SetScope` the same for tags; (2) "Only on this device" when the name exists hidden = a join (the picker/new-label form should say "'Work' is also on <device>" and give an explicit "Make shared", which is `SetOnlyOn(null)`); (3) pass the ADR 54 removed set where `removed: null` is passed today (PC `TaskActionsService`, `TaskStore` Place/ApplyLocal/ApplyCategories/ImportAsync; phone `ShelfEdits`/`TaskLocalEdits`/`PhoneTaskStore.import`); (4) the writers above already keep hidden labels and tags, so the screens only have to hide; a new writer must end with `KeepHidden`; (5) keep wiring `TaskScopes.LabelTest/TagTest` into the readers listed under "LB1a done".

### ST3 done (phone screen time v2: counting, derived entries, source sheet)

- **Scope:** `android/` only (app, shared, checks; wear and `essentials/*` untouched). No dependency, permission, connection or stored-shape change; Usage access is the existing grant. Not committed. Questions 1104-1109.
- **Counting:** `shared/goals/GoalScreenTimeDerived` (pure): for each live non-Deep-work goal with an automatic screen-time rule that counts on this phone (`GoalAppMatching.countsOn`), a day's value = whole minutes of the usage rows that ANY entry of the goal's counted rules matches (`GoalAppMatching.matches`, phone rules: Package exact, Name case-insensitive; Process / Website never on a phone); a row counts once. `app/goals/PhoneScreenTime` reads `PhoneUsageReader.read` per day for the last 7 days (closed days once per process, today at most every 5 min, never on the main thread, nothing without Usage access) into `GoalAppRow`s. `PhoneGoalDerived.refresh` (after each refresh and steps read) writes the values through `GoalDerivedWriter` / `PhoneGoalStore.writeDerived` with this phone's device id (same id, in place, only when the whole-minute value changed, never another device's entry). Other devices' entries are summed by the existing `GoalCounting.combined` (devices the rule counts on add up). `PhoneGoalData.build` has a new optional `screen` list: in-memory readings only for a goal and day with no stored entry (used by the goal editor's What it takes).
- **No-data rule:** a day with no usage rows is skipped (no 0, nothing removed); no `covered` ranges are passed so a read never removes a screen-time entry. Written with or without a group, like steps (GS4).
- **Source sheet:** `GoalSourcesSheet` Screen time row is live (was "Coming in the next update") and opens the new `ui/goals/ScreenTimeSourceSheet`: Usage access row (On / Off + "Open settings" to the system page; re-read on resume); the entries as a list (kind chip, tap cycles App id / Process / Name / Website; x removes; notes "Not seen on this device yet" / "Counts on PCs only"; app name shown for a package this phone knows); an add box that guesses the kind (`GoalAppMatchGuess`) with a "Counts as" chip and Add; a pick list "Seen on this phone" (launchable apps + 7 days of usage, fixed 240 dp height that scrolls, filters by what is typed, a tap adds a Package entry); "Counts from" device picker (this phone + linked PCs / phones, watches left out, at least one stays ticked, `createdOn` stamped with this phone's id on create, others enabled because ADR 79 is Accepted; flag `GoalScreenTimeEditor.OTHER_DEVICES_ENABLED`). The row opens the same page to edit the one source a goal has. `GoalDrafts.sourcesProblem`: a screen-time source needs at least one entry. Pure editor rules: `shared/goals/GoalScreenTimeEditor`.
- **SetupFeatures:** `goals.screenTime` (0.1.79, "Goals: screen time ... (needs Usage access)"), set up once seen or Usage access is on; assistant page `ui/ScreenTimeSetupPage` (what it does, Usage access row).

- **Hand test (phone, a test goal you create; delete it after):**
  1. Turn Usage access off for AccountabiliBuddy. Goals > + > name "ST3 test", Time, Daily, At most 60 min > Next > + Source > Screen time: the Usage access row says Off with "Open settings"; Create stays off, with "Add at least one app or site...", until an entry exists.
  2. Turn Usage access on in the system page and come back: the row says On.
  3. Type `com.instagram.android`: "Counts as App id"; Add. Type `YouTube`: Name. Type `youtube.com`: Website; tap the chip to cycle kinds; remove it with x. `com.nope.app` shows "Not seen on this device yet"; a Process or Website entry shows "Counts on PCs only".
  4. Pick list: scroll it, type "chr" to filter, tap an app: it joins the list as App id; tapping it again is disabled ("Already counting").
  5. Counts from: "This phone" is on and can't be switched off alone; tick a linked PC, then untick this phone (only the PC counts); tick this phone again.
  6. Add source, Create goal. Use a listed app for a few minutes, come back, wait one refresh (up to a few minutes): the goal shows whole minutes of the listed apps only.
  7. Edit goal > What counts > + Source > Screen time ("Change the apps, sites and devices"): the same list; Save keeps it.
  8. With a PC in the group and ticked: its minutes for the day add to the phone's (the PC half is ST2).
  9. Turn Usage access off: yesterday's numbers stay, nothing is overwritten with 0. Delete the test goal.

### ST2 done (PC screen time v2: counting from this PC's history, derived entries, editor)

- **Counting** (`Services/Goals/GoalScreenTimeSource.cs`, `GoalScreenTimeRows.cs`): one `GetSummaryAsync` per local day over `AppsAndSites`, matched through Core `GoalAppMatching` (a site row is offered with the program's shown name and the site's, counts once). Days before yesterday cached in memory; today and yesterday re-read each refresh (the 3-second goal-data coalescing already gates it). Deleted History time counts nowhere (cache cleared on `deletedUsage.Changed` / `AllDeleted`); renames only change matching (`displayNames.Changed` -> Recompute). Whole minutes. Nothing read unless a goal has a screen-time rule this PC counts. A failed read leaves `LoadedFrom` null: nothing written from a half-read history.
- **In-memory count** (`GoalSourceDataService.ScreenTime`, `WithReadings`): this PC's fresh readings replace its stored own entries in `Current` (same ids), group or not; the other devices' stored entries are summed by Core over `CountsOn` devices (unchanged Core rule).
- **Writing** (`GoalDerivedValuesService.AddScreenTime`): own id only, in a group only, only while the rule counts this PC, no write when unchanged, 90 days; a day with no logged activity is skipped unless today / already has an own entry (Q1112); own entries are tombstoned when the goal stops counting this PC.
- **Editor:** `GoalSourceRowViewModel` (entries list with kind ComboBox chips `GoalAppEntryViewModel`, Add box + Enter with guessed kind text, pick list `GoalSeenPickViewModel` from `GoalScreenTimeSeen` (this PC's 30 days, in memory, filtered by typed text, 84 px scrolling), "Counts from" `GoalDeviceOptionViewModel` list: this PC + group's other devices (watches left out), greyed without goals sync). `CreatedOn` stamped on create; an untouched device list is saved as it was (old rules keep "counts everywhere"); ids of devices that left stay. Screen time tile always offered (NG1 phone tiles unchanged); one screen-time source per goal. Text: "Measured on your phone" gone for screen time (row, tile, goal page line, day panel).
- **Wiring:** `App.axaml.cs` (source, events, device lookups), `GoalEditorLookups` (+Seen, SelfDevice*, OtherDevices, GoalsSyncOn). Docs: ADR 79 screen-time paragraph (PC clause), `doc/hard-rules.md` bullet.
- **Checks** (out of repo `tasks-ui-checks/AppChecks/St2Checks.cs`, fast run `ST2_ONLY=1`): 57 `st2/...` (counting per kind, once, deleted, write/no-churn/update/own-ids, sum over devices incl. not-counted and AllDevices, no names in entries or the sync file (seeded history-only names), no logging calls, editor guess/chip/pick/devices/old rule). Full AppChecks: see `tasks-ui-logs/st2-appchecks-full.log`; GoalsChecks 247/247. Mutations: Variants without the site label -> 1 fail; Core `CountsOn` filter removed -> 3 fail; both restored byte-for-byte. Shots `%TEMP%/claude/round2-shots-st2/` (901x567, 1440x880, tall 901 views; harness env `HARNESS_ST2=1`, `_SYNC=0`, `_TICK=1`, `_TYPED`; backup `GoalDialogs.cs.st2bak`): everything fits at 901 wide, no sideways scroll.

S1: `GoalScreenTimeDerived.readings(..., today, held)` updates only today and yesterday; an older day is written only when this phone has no live own entry for it (backfill); `PhoneGoalDerived` passes the trusted today and the held entries. The phone writes its own `ScreenTime` entry (device id on it, in place, only when the minute value changed) with or without a group, because its goal views count stored entries; a day with no usage records is no data (no 0, nothing removed), and an entry is never removed when a rule stops counting the phone (counting ignores it). The apps and sites a goal lists travel with the goal, sealed, to the user's own devices only; this phone's usage records and its pick list never leave it, and other devices are ticked in the editor only while 'Sync task lists' is on and the phone is in a group."

Nothing Kotlin, no `android/` edit, no new dependency, no connection, no permission. ADR 76 has a dated amendment line (2026-11-14); `doc/hard-rules.md` has "The task goal link" bullet and the `tasks` part list says it.
- **Core:** `TaskItem.GoalId` (JSON `goalId`, null-omitted, last parameter so no constructor call moves); `TaskField.Goal` (the 16th), `TaskFieldStamps.Goal` (JSON `goal`), `TaskFieldValues` canonical text = the goal id as `D` text or `""`, `With` copies it. `TaskActions.SetGoal` (+ `TaskToasts.Linked`), `TaskFieldStamps.ForNew(task, stamp)`. Goals: `GoalTaskLink` / `GoalTaskLinks.From(TaskLists)` (new), `GoalSourceData.TaskLinks` + `TaskIdsOf(goalId)`, `GoalFocusAttribution.Matches` / `FocusWindows` take the goal's task ids, `GoalSourceRule.CountsTime` includes `GoalField`, `GoalCounting` adds finished linked tasks to a milestone's day, `GoalSources.AvailabilityOf(GoalField / MilestoneTasks)` = `PcOnly` (Q1122).

- **Decisions (details in Q1118-Q1125):** a session counts for a linked task by the checklist task's **id** against the links as they are now (no session-record change); milestone progress counts finished tasks of the list + History's 90 days; Deep work can't be linked; `AllAt` leaves the goal unstamped and the merge leaves it unstamped while neither copy ever stamped it (a task with no link writes nothing new: text unchanged for older readers and the Kotlin parity fixtures); copies, History revival, undo, restore and tasks built from older devices' items use `ForNew`; a restore of a linked task stamps the link now, a backup task with no link never clears one.

### GS5b done (task goal link on the phone)

- **What it is:** `goalId` is a real field on the phone's task (`TaskItem.goalId`, `TaskField.GOAL`, stamp key `goal`) merged exactly like C#; the phone's task menu links and unlinks; both goal sources count on the phone from its task list; the goal page lists its tasks; a milestone's "Open its tasks" works. No new dependency, no connection, no permission, nothing under `essentials/`, no device touched, no `src/` edit.
- **Kotlin field and merge:** `TaskItem.goalId` (lower-case id, after `autoSavedFor`, before `extra`; written after `autoSavedFor`, null-omitted); `TaskFieldValues` canonical text = the id or `""`; `TaskFieldStamps.allAt` no longer stamps the goal and `TaskFieldStamps.forNew(task, stamp)` stamps it only when the task carries one (Q1125); `TaskFieldMerge` clears the picked stamp only when neither copy ever stamped it AND the values are equal (`goalNeverStamped`). The tie-break text (`SyncTaskItem.tieBreak`, the PC's JSON, known fields) now holds `goalId` and the goal stamp (Q1127 N2). `TaskItem.deleted` keeps no link (a tombstone). The phone stamps the goal only through `ShelfEdits.setGoal` (set or clear); no other writer makes a goal stamp for a task that never carried one.
- **Writer paths (each has a check, `gs5b/writers`):** `ShelfEdits.setGoal` / edits / move / finish / reopen / rename (stamps and link untouched), Undo (`restore` with `GOAL`), copies (`TaskHistoryRules.doAgain`, `TaskConflictActions.keepBothVersions`: Q1127 N3 done, both `forNew`), `bringBack` and `undoDelete` (`forNew`), `TaskListRules.restoredIncoming` (`forNew`: a restored link is stamped now, a backup task with no link writes none and never clears one made since), the older devices' lists (`TaskLocalEdits`: an edit keeps the link and its stamp; a tick then untick brings the task back from History with its link, `forNew`), `ShelfEdits.add` (no link). Not carried: a step made its own task (`ShelfSteps.ownTask`, Q1129; the PC has no such action) and the migration's tasks (they predate the field). The old-field writer (`OldTaskFieldsWriter`) carries no goal and never reads one. The phone has no session checklist writer of its own (the PC runs sessions), so there is no phone "held copy" path.
- **Counting:** `GoalSessionTask.id` (read from the checklist task's `id`), `GoalSourceData.taskLinks` + `taskIdsOf(goalId)`, `GoalTaskLink` / `GoalTaskLinks.from(TaskLists)` (open and finished tasks plus History's finished entries, once each), `GoalFocusAttribution.matches` / `focusWindows` take the goal's task ids (`GoalField` is a focus rule), `GoalCounting` adds finished linked tasks to a milestone's local day, `GoalDerivedMinutes` passes the ids. `GoalSources.availabilityOf(GoalField / MilestoneTasks)` = `Everywhere` (Q1130: the C# side must follow). The phone's builders (`PhoneGoalData.build`, `PhoneGoalPage`, `PhoneGoalsOverview`, the reminders, the What it takes box, Stats, Today's goals) all take the links (`rememberTaskLinks()` for screens, `PhoneGoalData.taskLinks(app)` for the reminders); a screen recounts only when a link, a title or a finish changed.
- **Q1126 flipped:** `gs5b/counting: Q1126` runs the S1 case on the Kotlin side: a tag + link goal (30 min linked and tagged, 10 tag only): the PC's #tag extra is 10 (the link's minutes left out), the PC shows 40 and the phone (the extra plus its own link count) shows the same 40; without the links it would show 10, the old gap. The C# `S1 known gap` check in `Gs5aGoalsChecks.cs` (outside the repo) pins only the PC side and the phone-less number, so it still passes; re-word it when the C# side follows Q1130.
- **Phone UI:** task ⋮ sheet: **Link to goal** after Tags (the goal's name at the right, "None" for none; greyed "No goals yet"), a page listing the live goals but Deep work (the goal's own icon, a check on the task's), a divider and **Unlink** (Link-off icon), one toast ("Linked to Spanish" / "Link removed") with Undo; select mode's Options has the same page for several tasks. The sources sheet: **A task carries this goal** (Link icon) and **Milestone tasks** (Checklist icon) are working rows under "ON THIS PHONE" (once each); Milestone tasks is greyed "Milestones only" unless the goal is a milestone (same rule as the PC tile). The goal page has a **Tasks** card (open first by title, then the latest finished, 8 rows, "2 of 5 finished" / "N more not shown" / "No task is linked yet. Open a task's menu › Link to goal"), shown when the goal has a task source or a linked task, never for Deep work; the old "Open its tasks, Coming soon" row is gone. The goal menu's **Open its tasks** (milestones) is enabled with "None linked yet" / "N linked" and shows the goal's page scrolled to the card (route anchor `tasks`; from the Goals list and from the page). **1. "At most" wording (both apps).** New shared words `GoalTexts.IsLimit / LimitLeft / LimitLeftSentence` (C# and Kotlin, same rule): "15 min left", "At the limit", "Over by 10 min" (Time, Amount, a day's or a period's limit; never a habit).
| Where | Before | Now |
|---|---|---|
| Goal page Today tile foot (Time, Amount) | "1h to go" | "15 min left" / "At the limit" / "Over by 10 min" |
| This period tile foot (weekly/monthly limit) | none | "4h 20m left" / "Over by 10 min" |
| Day panel status | "In progress · X to go", "Not counted · X short" | "In progress · 15 min left", "Over by 10 min" |
| Heatmap legend (minutes, daily) | "(1h+ counts)" | "(1h or less counts)" |
| Phone Today goals row | "70 of 60 · done" | "1h 10m of 1h · over by 10 min"; not counted in "n left" |
| Weekly review facts | "2 days behind", "Behind in 2 of the last 3" | "2 days over the limit", "Over the limit in 2 of the last 3" |
Counting: no Core change for it. The existing rule stays (today of an at-most goal is not hit until the day ends; a day is met when value <= target; weekly: met while total <= the prorated cap). "Days this week · on pace 0 of 7" is therefore literal (Q1151). Reminder texts use the neutral "45 min / 1h · On track" and needed no change.

**2. Beeminder vs kinds (`GoalCounting.cs` + `.kt`).** A hand entry of the day wins for every kind. Time: Beeminder value read as minutes (added to the other minutes) unless the day has a hand entry. Habit: ticked on a day value > 0, or >= the goal's threshold when it has one; the latest hand tick/untick still decides. Milestone: the day value is the count when there is no hand entry. Amount: unchanged. `doc/hard-rules.md` BM1 line says so. Edge calls in Q1150.

Fixtures: `goals/days` gained 13 cases (`fxc: ...`: at-most daily Time under / over / exactly at / nothing yet, at-most daily Amount, weekly 310 of 300 and 40 of 300, Beeminder on Time, Habit, Habit with threshold, Amount, Milestone, each with hand-vs-reading days); `goals-ui/detail`, `rows`, `week`, `reminder-done`, `stats` follow from them (65 cases each). **Repo changes (src only).** New `Core/Sync/GroupSessions.cs` (`IsLive(SyncFile, now)`, `LiveElsewhere(others, now)`, `StaleElsewhere(others, now)` for R3, `OpenPieceEnd(card, now)` for B4, `QuietAfter`), `GroupSessionLive.cs`, `GroupSessionStale.cs`. `OtherPcSessions` now delegates (still PC files only; behaviour proved identical in 528 combinations against a copy of the old rule). B4 clamp on the receiving side: `ReceivedSessions.Closed/In` got an optional `now` (all 6 callers now pass `TimeProvider.GetUtcNow()`), `SyncMerge.Merge` closes a stored phone copy's open piece at `min(writtenAt, now)`. PC files are read exactly as before.

**For PS1b.** Fixtures `%TEMP%/claude/round2-fixtures/ps1/{live,pieces,tasks,end-at-once,merge,overlap}.json` (shape `{area,name,source,cases:[{name,input,expected}]}`; times are ISO text with offsets).
- `live`: input `{now, files:[SyncFile JSON]}`; expected `{isLive:[bool per file], live:{deviceId,name,kind,endsAt,paused,noSetEnd}|null, stale:{deviceId,name,lastHeardAt}|null}`. Rules: PC file = the old PC rule (planning, 15 min quiet, no-set-end 30 min, never away). Phone file live iff `offSince` null AND `now - min(writtenAt, now) <= 15 min` (boundary inclusive) AND (`phaseEndsAt` null OR `awaySince` set OR `now < phaseEndsAt`). Watch never live. `endsAt = phaseEndsAt + max(0, now - awaySince)`; `paused = awaySince != null`; `noSetEnd = phaseEndsAt == null`. Several live: newest `writtenAt` (raw), tie the lower deviceId text (ordinal). `stale` (R3): newest-written phone file with `running`, no `offSince`, quiet over 15 min (clamped age), and the phase still on by the same test; PC never stale.
- `pieces`: input `{now, zoneOffsetMinutes:-300, running, oldestSessionStart, kindMap, records:[{startedAt,endedAt|null,profile,away:[{kind,from,to|null}],tasks}]}`, newest record first as given; kinds `StepAway,Paused,Lunch,DeviceOff` (C# `Break,Paused,Paused,DeviceOff`). Expected `{sessions, sessionsText, sessionsTextWithoutIsDone, coversFrom}` from the real `SyncFileBuilder.Build`. Rules: ended record cuts every away stretch; running record cuts only DeviceOff, last piece open (`end` null); checklist on piece 0 only; `offMinutesBefore` (rounded minutes, >= 1) on the piece after a DeviceOff gap; `profile` = name, `added` false; sessions that end before the local midnight before `now - 14 d` are dropped; `coversFrom` = that midnight when `oldestSessionStart` is older than it, else `now - 90 d`. Compare PARSED JSON and ignore the key `isDone`: the C# writer adds the computed `isDone` to every task and step, the Kotlin writer does not and readers ignore it. Optional keys are left out when null in C# except `end` (written as `null`).
- `tasks`: input `{json:[tasks]}`, expected `{readable, json, text, textWithoutIsDone}`. Task keys `id,title,doneAt,subtasks,categoryId,category,categoryColor,labels,localTo`; `labels` absent when none. Ids must be GUID text (a non-GUID makes the PC refuse the whole file: `readable:false`; B11). Upper-case ids come back lower-case. A local task is a stub (`title:""`, `localTo`).
- `end-at-once`: input `{sessionStart, madeBeforeStart, droppedOpen, tasks:[{id,doneAt,subtasks:[{id,doneAt}]}], now}`, expected `{canEndAtOnce, allDone}` = `AllDone && madeBeforeStart && sessionStart != null && now - sessionStart >= 10 min`; `AllDone` = has tasks, `droppedOpen == 0`, every TASK has `doneAt` (steps are not looked at: a ticked task with an open step still passes, case included). Boundary cases at 10:00 / 9:59 / 10:01. Removing or parking an open task adds 1 to `droppedOpen` (B6); removing a done task does not.
- `merge` (B5): `{now, storedStarts, incomingWrittenAt, incomingCoversFrom, incomingStarts}` -> `{keptStarts}` from the real `SyncMerge.Merge`. An unreadable log must write no `running`, `sessions: []` and `coversFrom == writtenAt` (receivers keep everything); `coversFrom = now - 90 d` with no sessions erases the receiver's history (a case shows it).
- `overlap` (written by AppChecks): input `{now, zoneOffsetMinutes, days, own:[{start,end,added}], files, ...}`; expected per day `focusedSeconds` (union across devices), `longestSeconds`, `budgetSeconds` (no added sessions, own and others), `unlockSeconds` (OTHER devices only, no added), plus `coveredSeconds` / `extraSeconds`; the last case has `labelId`, `goalTaskIds` and `goalFocusMinutes {all,label,goalTask}` (`FromSynced` joins pieces: no tasks, same profile, starts within 60 min of the previous piece's end). An open piece ends at `min(writtenAt, now)` for a phone.

**Not in PS1a (for later tasks).** PS4 wires `GroupSessions.LiveElsewhere` into start paths. `OtherPcSessions` is still PC-only (the lunch auto-start); PS4 switches it. The Kotlin twin of the clamp must also apply in `FocusWindows` and the phone's own receive path.

**For PS2a (the runtime) and later.**
- **Engine API** (`PhoneSessionEngine`, all pure, every `now` a trusted time; each returns `PhoneSessionResult.Done(record, effects)` or `Refused(reason)`): `start(id, profileId, name, lengthMinutes?, now, tasks, isTest)`, `stepAway/back`, `pause/resume`, `lunchHold(record, from, now)/lunchEnd(record, now)`, `add10`, `tick(record, now)` (time up at the END moment, armed end applies), `heartbeat`, `phoneOff` (saves `offAt`), `applyOffGap(record, decision, now)`, `openEnd` (the wait counts from here), `endEarly(record, now, mode, settings, reason)` (arms before the wait, ends after), `cancelEnd`, `applyArmed`, `endAtOnce`, `endOnCommand`, `checklistChanged(record, tasks)` (counts drops, B6). Effects to carry out: `Persist, WriteSyncFile, ScheduleEndAlarm(at?), ScheduleArmedAlarm(at?), EnforceUntil(endsAt?), ReleaseEnforcement(kind), SaveReason(text), OffGapNotice`. A running record is `endedAt == null`: `isRunning` takes no clock (B1); only `tick` on a trusted time ends one. The mode is passed per call (read live, S3).
- **Off gap (R13):** `PhoneSessionOffGap.decide(record, now, newBoot, timeIsTrusted, zone)` then `applyOffGap`. `newBoot` = `BOOT_COUNT` changed OR `offAt` was saved (same boot, no `offAt` = no gap, B3a). `timeIsTrusted` needs a flag from `TrustedTime` (it has none today, B3b): until then pass `false` when unsure (only pauses). Checkpoint must keep `heartbeatAt` and `offAt` (they are fields of the record).
- **Sync file:** `SessionPieces.build(records, now, zone, oldestSessionStart)` -> `Built(sessions, coversFrom)`; `SessionPieces.runningOf(record)`; unreadable log -> `whenLogUnreadable(writtenAt)` and write NO `running` (B5). Stamp `writtenAt` from `PhoneSessionClock`, never the wall clock (B4). The checklist's tasks must have GUID ids (non-GUID ones are left out by `forSync`, because the PC refuses the whole file); a task kept local to this device carries `localTo = <this device id>` and goes as a stub. Test records never go.
- **Receiving:** every reader of an open piece uses `GroupSessions.openPieceEnd(device, now)`; `FocusWindows.of(files, zone, now)` needs `now` now. Q1152 (stamp ahead of now) lives only in that one function. `GroupSessions.liveElsewhere/staleElsewhere(others, now, pcLive)` for R2/R3: pass `PcFileControl.liveRunning` as `pcLive` on the phone (Q1158); `GroupSessions.pcRuleOfCore` is the PC's own rule (what the fixtures pin).
- **Locks:** `PhoneSessionLocks.scope(mode, record)` gives `PhoneLockArea`s (AB: all; PB: LMP/Essentials rows, Curbox rows, session profiles, end-early setting); `stricter(a, b)` for a PC and an own session at once. Map each existing `PlacementSession.current` / `GuardsLock` consumer to an area (B9 table is PS2c's).
- **End terms:** `PhoneSessionEndRules.terms(mode, record, settings)`; defaults 60 s / 15 characters; none for PB, no set end, test. Reasons: effect `SaveReason` (sealed, 90 days, never synced or logged; `toString` of the armed end and the effect leave it out).
- **Checklist:** `PhoneSessionChecklist` holds the tasks for R9/B6 only (ticks go through `tickTask/tickStep`, or the task store then `checklistChanged`). `canEndAtOnce` = all tasks ticked (steps not looked at, Q1154) + none dropped + made before start + 10 min.

- **PS1a fix round** (Opus review `ps1a-review.md`; not committed): solution build 0 errors; Core 2076/2076 (295 `ps1:`); GoalsChecks 307/307; AppChecks 1510/1510 (57 `ps1:`); the three programs run one at a time (no timing flake). 21 mutations, each fails at least one `ps1:` check and is restored byte for byte (hash checked; `ps1a_mutations.py`). Fixtures regenerated last in `%TEMP%/claude/round2-fixtures/ps1/` (now also `checklist-ops.json`).
  Files changed in this round (repo, src only): `Core/Sync/{GroupSessions,SyncMerge,SyncFileBuilder}.cs`, new `Core/Sync/ReceivedSyncFile.cs`, `Core/Domain/{SessionTask,SessionChecklist}.cs`, `App/Services/Sync/{ReceivedSessions,OtherPcSessions,ReceivedDevicesService,SyncCoordinator}.cs`, `App/App.axaml.cs`, `App/ViewModels/{DayBar,Devices,History,Stats}ViewModel.cs`.

  **For PS1b: exactly what changed since "PS1a done" (these REPLACE the earlier rules where they differ).**
  1. **Phone files are judged on `heardAt = min(writtenAt, receivedAt)`** (`receivedAt` = the receiver's trusted arrival time of that device's newest file; Kotlin `ReceivedDevice.receivedAt`). PC files: unchanged, `receivedAt` unused. API: `GroupSessions.IsLive(file, receivedAt, now)`, `LiveElsewhere(IEnumerable<ReceivedSyncFile>, now)`, `StaleElsewhere(...)`, `HeardAt(card, receivedAt)`, `OpenPieceEnd(file, receivedAt, now)`.
  2. **Live (phone):** `offSince` null AND `writtenAt <= now + 2 min` (a stamp more than 2 min ahead of the trusted now is NOT live; exactly +2:00 is live) AND `now - heardAt <= 15 min` (inclusive) AND (`phaseEndsAt` null OR `awaySince` set OR `now < phaseEndsAt`). `endsAt = phaseEndsAt + max(0, now - awaySince)`; a future `awaySince` adds nothing.
  3. **Stale (R3):** newest-heard phone file with `running`, no `offSince`, `15 min < now - heardAt <= 4 h` (new bound: older than 4 h is taken as over, nothing shown), and the phase test above. `lastHeardAt` is `heardAt`, not the stamp. A phone stamped far ahead that just arrived is neither live nor stale. Winner among several live or several stale: newest `heardAt`, tie the lower deviceId text (ordinal).
  4. **Open piece (phone):** ends at `min(heardAt, now, running.awaySince if set)`; it does not grow with `now`. Applies in `Closed`, `In`, the merge's closing of a stored open piece, and the goal/focus readers. A phone piece that starts after that end is left out. PC: `writtenAt`, as before (a PC's current pause is NOT cut; see Q1162).
  5. **Merge:** `SyncMerge.Merge(stored, incoming, now, storedReceivedAt)`: an incoming file is refused as older only when its stamp is earlier than `HeardAt(stored, storedReceivedAt)`, so an honest file after a skewed one is taken; the stored copy's open piece closes at `OpenPieceEnd(stored, storedReceivedAt ?? now, now)`. The PC's refusal in `SyncCoordinator` uses the same `HeardAt(stored.File.Device, stored.ReceivedAt)`. The `now` of `ReceivedSessions.Closed/In` is now required.
  6. **Writer, every device (B1, owner OK "cut paused time"):** a RUNNING record cuts Planning, DeviceOff AND every away stretch that has finished (`to <= now`: step away, pause, lunch, anything); only a stretch still going stays in the open piece. Ended records still cut everything. A stretch that just ended at `now` gives a closed piece then an empty open piece `[now, null]`. Fixture `pieces` cases "running with a finished ...", "paused now only", "just ended", "lunch finished", "step away finished". Receivers cut the current pause by item 4 (phone files carry `awaySince` for Pause timer, lunch, device off, never for Step away, ADR 77).
  7. **S1 rounding:** `offMinutesBefore` = minutes of the DeviceOff gap, key only when >= 1 minute, rounded HALF UP (`Math.round`; C# now `MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero`, it was banker's): 0:59 none, 1:00 -> 1, 30:30 -> 31, 31:30 -> 32. Fixture `pieces` "S1".
  8. **S2:** fixture `merge` "S2": `sessions: []` with `coversFrom: null` speaks from `writtenAt - 14 d` and erases the last 14 days; the phone must write `coversFrom = writtenAt` explicitly (never null) for an unreadable log. New merge input fields `storedWrittenAt`, `storedReceivedAt` (two B2 cases).
  9. **S3:** new `checklist-ops.json`: `{sessionStart, now, taskIds, ops:[{op: tick|remove|park|undo, id, at}]}` -> `steps:[{step, droppedOpen, openTaskIds, allDone, canEndAtOnce}]` for the start and after each op. `tick` toggles; `remove` / `park` of an OPEN task add 1 (a ticked one adds 0); `undo` puts the last removed or parked task back at its index and, if it was open, takes 1 off.
  10. **S4:** a task kept local to a device goes in a session piece as its stub (`title: ""`, no `subtasks`, `localTo`), PC wiring `SessionTask.ForSyncFile()` in `syncCoordinator.TasksOf`. Fixture `pieces` "S4". (The phone has no local tasks yet; copy the rule only if it gets them.)
  11. **Q1154:** the rule stays: `CanEndAtOnce` looks only at each task's `doneAt`; the Kotlin twin must keep the invariant "unticking a step or adding one clears the task's tick". Doc wording on `AllDone` fixed (the plan's R9 text "with every step" is the plan's to fix in PS7).
  12. **Fixture shape changes:** `live` and `overlap` inputs carry a parallel `receivedAt` array (one ISO time per file); `live` expected `stale.lastHeardAt` is heardAt. Comparison note (N7): compare parsed JSON, ignore the key `isDone`, and treat an absent key like `null` (C# omits optional keys, the Kotlin writer writes nulls).
  **Review nits:** done N1 (order by heardAt), N2 (4 h stale bound), N4 (stored copy bound by its own arrival time), N5 (14-day edge case), N6 (odd phone `running` shapes, +1/+2/+3 min ahead), N8 (lastHeardAt = heardAt). Skipped: N3 (PC rule has no away exception: pre-existing, left; PS4 must know R1 is asymmetric for a paused PC), N7 (a comparison note only, above).

Fixtures regenerated last. Files changed in this round (repo): `Core/Sync/{GroupSessions,SyncFileBuilder}.cs` only (everything else as in the first fix round).
  **Changes to the For PS1b list (these REPLACE items 1, 3, 4 and 6 of the first fix round where they differ; the rest stands):**
  - **6 (writer, every device): a record, running or ended, cuts EVERY away stretch, including one going now.** An ongoing stretch (`to` = still open) ends the piece before it at the stretch's start (Step away needs no `awaySince`: the writer's own start time is enough); then, as for a stretch that just ended, an EMPTY open piece `[now, null]` follows (start = the write time, zero length at the receiver). So a running session that is paused / at lunch / stepping away now has: closed pieces up to the stretch start + an empty open piece. Pieces only get shorter; older apps just count less. Fixture `pieces`: "paused now only", "step away going now", "lunch going now", the finished-stretch cases (all updated), expected from the real `SyncFileBuilder.Build`.
  - **4 (receiver, ANY device): the open piece ends at `min(heardAt, now, running.awaySince if set)`** for PC files too (a writer that doesn't cut yet, e.g. an older PC; the piece is dropped when that end is not after its start). Overlap fixtures: "an older PC writer ... cut at 10:40", "a pause going now ... counts nothing".
  - **1 (`heardAt`, ANY device): `heardAt = min(writtenAt, receivedAt)` for PC files as well.** It is used for: the age in the PC live rule (`now - heardAt > 15 min` is quiet; the no-set-end 30 minute freshness), the open piece end (4), the refusal of an incoming file as older (`incoming.writtenAt < heardAt(stored)`, in `SyncMerge` and `SyncCoordinator`) and the stored copy's open-piece close. On an honest clock (the file arrives at or after its stamp) the PC rule is exactly the old one: the 528-combination check still passes (with received = stamp, and received = stamp + 7 min). The PC live rule still has NO ahead tolerance and no other change; a PC stamped ahead that arrived long ago is judged by its arrival (not live), one that just arrived is live as before. Fixture `live` PC cases: "arrived a little after it was written", "stamp 10 minutes ahead, arrived 20 minutes ago: not live", "... arrived just now: live".
  - 3 (stale) and 2 (phone live) unchanged. Phone only: the +2 min ahead tolerance, the stale line and its 4 h bound.

  **What the Kotlin side must mirror:**
  1. **The open piece carries the checklist again (no format change).** In the running session's pieces, `tasks` is on piece 0 AND on the open piece (`end` null), the same list. Every other piece has none. Ended sessions: piece 0 only, as before. C# rule: `index == 0 || (running && piece.End >= counted.End) ? tasks : null`. A running session with a single open piece is unchanged. Fixture `pieces` is regenerated (every running case with a checklist: the open piece now has `tasks`). Goal attribution (`FromSynced`) is unchanged and needs no code change: pieces that carry tasks start a goal session; the union counts no minute twice; so "a label goal counts the running session's own pieces" is as before the pause cut. Readers: `runningTasks = visible(sessions.lastOrNull { end == null }.tasks)`, no walk.
  2. **New optional field `running.sessionStartedAt`** (ISO timestamp with offset, same format as `phaseStartedAt`): the start time of the running session. JSON: key `sessionStartedAt`, the LAST key of `running` (after `offSince`), omitted when null; older readers ignore it, a file without it reads as null (keep `null` and absent equal). C# `SyncRunning(..., OffSince, SessionStartedAt)`. Value: `SyncFileBuilder.RunningSessionStart(records)`: the start of the newest recorded session that is not added by hand, only when that record has no end; null otherwise (never for a test session: `running` itself is null then). The phone writer: the running record's `startedAt`.
  3. **Readers key by it:** `session key = running.sessionStartedAt ?: <the old fallback>` (phone: `PlacementSession.fromPc`, work-use friction `WorkUseStore` / `WorkUseChooser`, `PlacementStore.begin`). C# reader changed: `PcPickupsService.PickupsForOtherPcs` keys another PC's open piece by `running.sessionStartedAt` (when it is not after the piece's start) and counts pickups from it, so the pickup's `sessionStart` is stable while the user is away. Fixture `live` has three cases with `sessionStartedAt` present: the live and stale rules ignore it.
  4. Fixture `pieces` input/expected gains `expected.runningSessionStartedAt` (ISO, or null when the case is not running): the Kotlin `runningOf` must give `record.startedAt` exactly when the record is the running one.

### PS2a done (phone session runtime: clock, sealed store, controller, alarms, boot path; `android/` only, not committed)
Build `assembleDebug :checks:run --offline --no-daemon`: green, 4721/4721 (134 `ps2a`). 38 mutations (script `scratchpad/ps2a_mutations.py`, out of repo), each fails 1 or more checks (most 2-9), each restored byte for byte (sha256 asserted): clock on the wall clock, same-boot restart making a gap (B3a), untrusted gap as trusted (B3b), untrusted heartbeat at a state change / at refresh / at shutdown (V-B1), the engine's untrusted heartbeat, codec fields dropped (droppedOpenIds, heldAway, networkBacked), unreadable read as missing (B5), no history marker, plain-text store, aside copy overwritten, lunch end never seen (B2), lunch from first seen (Q1166), resume ignoring a lunch (Q1165), no R1 at start, no re-anchor at start, failed save ignored, effects before the save, no alarms at app start, end alarm kept while paused, armed alarm never set, reason not kept, heartbeat redraw loop, hold never decided, network time never upgrading, B6 changes not applied, mode ignored, a wall clock in the controller (scan), log/reasons never pruned, reboot not detected, test session logged, alarm on the wall clock. One (mode ignored) kills only 1 check.

**Files (all `android/`).** New, `shared/.../session/`: `PhoneSessionAnchor`, `PhoneSessionClock` (+ `PhoneClockSource`, `PhoneTimeReading`), `PhoneSessionData` (checkpoint, notice, store data, `PhoneSessionRead` Missing/Unreadable/Readable, reason), `PhoneSessionCodec`. New, `app/.../session/`: `PhoneSessionStore` (File + a handed-in sealer, pure), `PhoneSessionSealer`, `PhoneSessionPorts` (+ `PhoneAlarmKind`), `PhoneSessionStart`, `PhoneSessionController` (the only writer; pure), `PhoneSessionRuntime` (wiring), `PhoneSessionAlarm`, `PhoneSessionAlarmReceiver`, `AndroidPhoneClockSource`, `KeystorePhoneSessionSealer` (alias `accountabilibuddy-phone-session`). New check `checks/.../Ps2aSelfCheck.kt`. Edited: `shared/.../PhoneSessionEngine.kt` (`applyOffGap(..., timeIsTrusted = true)`, Q1169), `guidance/TrustedTime.kt` (`reading()`, `isNetworkBacked()`, `now()` unchanged), `CompanionApp.kt` (`phoneSession` + `start()` at the end of `onCreate`), `notify/Refresher.kt` (`app.phoneSession.refresh()` at the top of each refresh), `AndroidManifest.xml` (the alarm receiver), `checks/build.gradle.kts` (the five pure app files), `checks/.../Main.kt`. Stage the new files with `git add`. Questions Q1166-Q1172.

**What it does.** `PhoneSessionClock` = anchor (trusted wall, elapsed, boot count, network-backed) taken at a start and after each reboot; `now = wall + elapsed delta`, so the phone's clock moves nothing; the anchor is saved with the checkpoint (a same-boot restart carries on). `PhoneSessionStore`: sealed `files/session/phone-sessions.sealed` (checkpoint + 90-day log of ended records + notice + `historyStartsAt`) and `phone-session-reasons.sealed` (90 days), atomic tmp + move, "can't open" = `Unreadable` (a copy `.unreadable` is set aside, never over an earlier one, and the new log carries `historyStartsAt`). Controller: `start` (R1 `liveElsewhere` through the group rule, new anchor, save first or `StoreFailed`), every user action returns the engine's answer, `tick`, `refresh` (network time re-anchors; a Hold is decided again; lunch; end; trusted heartbeat), `onStart` (reboot = boot count changed or `offAt`; B3a same-boot restart = no gap; off gap judged with the clock's trust; alarms and enforcement asked again), `onShutdown` (trusted clock only), `observeLunch` (pause from the lunch's recorded start, ends when first seen not running), `thisSessionChanged/Undone` (B6). Effects after the save: alarms follow the RECORD (no end alarm while the countdown is paused; the armed alarm), `requestSyncWrite`, `enforceUntil`, `releaseEnforcement`, sealed reason. Alarms count on the ELAPSED clock (exact when allowed). `changed()` is not called for a heartbeat alone.

**For PS2b (sync and counting).**
- Hook `app.phoneSession.onSyncWrite` (called after each save for a real session, never a test). Read `controller.running`, `controller.log` (ended, no test), `controller.now()` for `SessionPieces.build(log + running, now, zone, oldestSessionStart)`; stamp `writtenAt` with `controller.now()`, not a wall clock.
- `controller.logUnreadable` true: `SessionPieces.whenLogUnreadable(writtenAt)` and no `running`. `controller.historyStartsAt` non-null: `coversFrom = max(built.coversFrom, historyStartsAt)` (B5 after a lost log).
- B13: 5-minute rewrite while a session runs on every sync method; `Refresher` already refreshes about every 5 min and calls `controller.refresh()` (which saves but does not write the file).
- `running` in the file: an open DEVICE_OFF stretch carries `awaySince` (also while a Hold); Step away never does (ADR 77).
- `app.phoneSession.running` (StateFlow) is the thing for `focusFiles`; counting must never compare it with a caller's `now` (B1).
**For PS2c.** Hooks `onEnforceUntil(record, endsAt)` and `onRelease(record, kind)` on the runtime (asked at start, resume, +10, app start; release at every end incl. PHONE_OFF). `isRunning` takes no clock. The lock seam reads `controller.running` and `PhoneSessionLocks.scope(mode, record, profileUse)`. Q1167 (start on a movable clock) and Q1170 are the open design points.
**For PS3a.** Q1171 (This session list to the controller) and Q1172 (no entry point yet).

**What was built (shapes, exactly).**
- `SyncFile.DeviceRoles` (JSON `deviceRoles`, appended after `tagScopes`), then `SyncFile.StepsTracking` (`stepsTracking`). Both null-omitted, both lenient (`SyncLenientListConverter<SyncDeviceRole>`, `SyncLenientObjectConverter<SyncStepsTracking>`). ForQr keeps roles, drops the switch. `KeptCopy` untouched (the kept copy may hold both).
- `SyncDeviceRole(Id, Role, At, By)` keys `id, role, at, by`; role words `main|tablet|other` (`SyncDeviceRole.RoleMain/RoleTablet/RoleOther`), unknown words kept, behave as other. `SyncDeviceRoles.Merge(held, incoming, now, removed)` (one entry per id; newest `at`; tie: higher `by`, then higher role word, ordinal D text / ordinal; stamp capped at now + 2 min and the capped value stored; entries with no id or no role word skipped; removed ids dropped; id order; null when empty); `With(held, id, role, at, by)` (a main demotes every other main to `other` at the same stamp and writer); `Effective(entries, androidIds)` -> `SyncDeviceRoleKind` Main|Tablet|Other|Unassigned (winner = newest `main` of an active Android device; tie: higher `by`, then higher id; other mains Other; no entry: Other when a winner exists else Unassigned; several entries per id = the newest).
- `SyncStepsTracking(On, At, By)` keys `on, at, by`; `Merge(held, incoming, now)` (newest; tie: higher `by`, then OFF wins; capped); `ForUpgrade(localOn, self)` (on -> `2000-01-01T00:00:00Z`, off/fresh -> null); `UpgradeStamp`.
- `GoalSourceRule.StepsFrom` (`GoalStepsFrom { Watch, Phone }`, JSON `"stepsFrom":"Watch"`, after `createdOn`, before unknown fields, null omitted, `GoalEnumConverter`: unknown value = kept newer item). `GoalEntry.StepsFrom` (B1: the class actually used, appended after `deviceId`, null omitted). `GoalDerivedReading.StepsFrom` + `GoalDerivedWriter`: a new entry carries the word, and a changed word rewrites the entry like a changed value (so switching All/Watch/Phone with the same count still updates).
- `GoalCounting`: (1) the synced `steps` total counts only while no automatic steps rule has `stepsFrom`; (2) inside the cover (`GoalCounting.StepsCoverDays` = 30: today and the 29 days before) a goal with `stepsFrom` set counts only Health Connect entries that carry a word; a word-less entry on an older day still counts (Q1177). No entry-word vs rule-class comparison.
- `GoalAppMatch.Devices` (JSON `devices` after `text`, null omitted, empty list kept = counts nowhere). `GoalAppMatching.CountsOn(rule, match, device)`, `MinutesFor(rule, rows, platform, device)` (entries filtered first; the old 3-argument overload is unchanged), `OneEntryPerKindAndText(entries)` (twins by exact kind+text join their lists; a twin with no list = everywhere; same list returned when nothing merges), `CountsNowhere(match, countedDevices)` (editor warning).
- ADR 90 and ADR 91 written as Accepted with the owner's words; ADR 79 got the dated ST4 line. ADR 90 owns the Drive gate (Q1178).

ST4: call `OneEntryPerKindAndText` when saving a rule's entries (B7), `CountsOn(rule, match, device)` and `MinutesFor(..., device)` in `GoalScreenTimeSource` / `GoalDerivedValuesService.AddScreenTime` (pass this PC's id), `CountsNowhere` for the warning.

### PS2b done (phone sessions sync and counting; `android/` only, not committed)
Build `assembleDebug :checks:run --offline --no-daemon`: green, 4881/4881 (115 `ps2b`; PS2a/PS1 unchanged apart from one pin: the waking alarm is 5 minutes now, see Q1180). 46 mutations (`scratchpad/ps2b_mutations.py`, out of repo), each fails at least 1 real check, each restored byte for byte (sha256 asserted by the script; `FAIL  ` lines counted, compile errors flagged): B4 stamp from the wall clock (25 checks), B5 unreadable log not silent, B3b an unstampable clock still writes, V-N1 unchecked running session sent (pieces, card), unchecked ended sent, B5 coversFrom ignores the history start, own counting on the 14-day window, own counting skipping unchecked, own session counted twice, own session never counted (4), own stamp not the session clock, B13 write every 30 min / never / for a test session / alarm back to 15 min / state change asks for no write (9), V-N1 ends never marked / only the start marked / correction not moving the record / mark not cleared / old anchor kept after a new start / no write at the correction, `syncInput` stampable always / never unchecked / unchecked below Android 13, ownFile stamped with the wall clock / without `running` / `sessions` / `coversFrom`, pickups counting phones, no 2-minute heartbeat in a phone session, Drive and the relay not busy, the folder not asked, the work-event credit / day header / reminders / Today-Goals-Stats feed / watch total ignoring own sessions, arrival time not passed (goal page, overview, watch snapshot), `arrivalOf` returning the stamp, write gate heartbeat 30 min, codec dropping the marks, the writer naming the profile id. The first pass left 4 alive (an end on an unchecked clock only; an old anchor after a new start; no write at a small correction; `arrivalOf`) and one did not compile (own never counted): all got a check or a better mutation. One more (`correctable.clear()` at a reboot) was dead code (a reboot kills the process that holds it) and was removed.

**What the phone writes now (all `android/`).**
- `shared/.../session/PhoneSessionSyncWriter.kt` (new, pure): `PhoneSessionSyncInput` (the controller's snapshot) -> `PhoneSessionSyncPart(writtenAt, running, sessions, coversFrom)`. `forFile(input, fallbackNow)`: stamped with the SESSION clock (B4), never the wall clock; `SessionPieces.build` cuts exactly as the PC does (the `pieces` fixture, end to end, all 24 cases); `running` = `SessionPieces.runningOf` (profile NAME, phase, set end, `awaySince` for Pause timer / lunch / device off only, `sessionStartedAt`; no profile id, no app list, no end reason); **silent** (no `running`, no sessions, `coversFrom = writtenAt`, never null) when the log can't be opened (B5) or the clock can't stamp yet (Android 13+, no network time); `coversFrom = max(built, historyStartsAt)` after a lost log; V-N1: a running or ended session whose times are not corrected yet is left out. `forCounting(input)`: every session the phone holds (the 90-day log; the 14-day file window does not apply), unchecked ones too.
- `PhoneSessionController`: `syncInput()` (one snapshot under the lock). B13: `requestWrite()` after every state change the engine reports AND from `refresh()` when 4 minutes have passed since the last ask while a real session runs (never a test); the waking alarm is 5 minutes (was 15, Q1180). V-N1: a start on an unchecked clock (Android 13+) sets `runningUnchecked`; an ended session that started or ended unchecked is marked in the store (`PhoneSessionStoreData.uncheckedEnded`, in the codec); the network time arriving corrects the running session (and asks for the file) or, within the same process and anchor, the marked log records (`withCorrected`); a record that can't be corrected (the process died, a new start re-anchored) stays marked and out of the file (Q1181).
- `SyncCoordinator`: `var ownSessions` (hook); `ownFile()` takes the part once: the card's `writtenAt`, `sessions`, `running`, `coversFrom` come from it (no hook = as before). `pickups()` unchanged (still skips phones). `inFocusSession` is true during a phone session too (the write gate's heartbeat is 2 min), `driveBusy()` is true (Drive and the relay at 30 s), and `CompanionApp.sendOwnFileNow()` (folder + Drive + relay) is the `onSyncWrite` hook: the folder is written on session changes and at least every 4 minutes whatever its timer says.
- `session/PhoneSessionFiles.kt` (new, pure): `ownDevice(input, id, name, version)` = the phone's own sessions as a `ReceivedDevice` (stamp = arrival = the session clock, so an open piece ends at the session's now whatever the reader's clock says, B1/B4); `focusFiles(received, own)` replaces any copy of this phone's own id. `CompanionApp.focusDevices()` is the door.

**Counting (B9, classified).** Focus readers use `app.focusDevices()` or the focus flow: `countedFocusWindows` (Work-event credit), `HeaderSource.focus` (day header, watch), `GoalReminders`, `WatchBridge` (`focusOf`), `CompanionViewModel.syncFocusReceived` (replaces `syncAllReceived`; recomputed every 30 s while a session runs) -> MainScreen -> Today goals, Goals, Stats, the Devices day line. Device lists stay on `sync.allReceived`, and a check pins the complete set of files that read it: `CompanionApp` (goal history devices; the door), `CompanionViewModel` (a device's name; the flow's source), `RemoveDeviceFlow`, `ScopeViews`, `ArchiveData`. `PhoneGoalPage`, `PhoneGoalsOverview` and `PhoneSnapshot.input` got `receivedAtOf` (the PS1b known limit is closed); every caller passes the arrival time (`List<ReceivedDevice>.arrivalOf()`).

A stamp more than 2 minutes ahead is DROPPED, not capped (roles and switch). `With` returns entries stamped with the NextStamp rule (instants, one tick = 100 ns in C#; keep the same granularity: the fixtures use `.AddTicks(1)`, so write them with 7-digit fractional seconds). `Effective`, `MainChanged`, `SyncDeviceRoleGate` and the per-day `ForClass` rule are all in fixtures. `GoalCounting.StepsCoverDays` no longer exists.

Count the delay from the first time this device saw the change (a device-local time kept with the pending state), not from the entry's `at`. The Drive gate applies to every role change and reads the device's own send log through `SyncDeviceRoleGate` (PC: `SyncSendLog` per method, newest `WrittenAt`; phone: its `SyncSendLog`); it applies on this device only; a derived change (removal, leaving) has no gate, only the delay. - **PS2b fix round** (Opus `ps2b-review.md`; `android/` only, not committed): `assembleDebug :checks:run --offline --no-daemon` green, 4909/4909 (the PS2b checks are now 143). 64 mutations in `scratchpad/ps2b_mutations.py` (out of repo; the earlier risky ones re-run and 24 new for B1, S1-S5, Q1181, Q1183, N2, N4, N5), each fails at least 1 real check and each file is restored byte for byte (sha256 asserted). Survivors of the first pass (boot not compared at the correction, an earlier boot's start moved by a later boot's correction, below-13 history start only set by a later call) got checks. Counts in the first PS2b entry's mutation line were "printed fails"; the script counted the `FAILURE: Build failed` line too, so those numbers were one too high; this round counts `FAIL  ` lines only.
  **B1 done.** `PhoneSessionStoreData.historyUnknown` (in the codec) is saved with the new log whenever no history start can be set; on load `logUnreadable = historyUnknown && no historyStartsAt`, cleared in the same write that sets `historyStartsAt` (`markHistoryWhenTrusted` now uses `canBeat()`, so below Android 13 it never waits). Below 13 the history start is set by the very start from the lost log. A MISSING log with a set-aside copy beside it (`PhoneSessionStore.hasAsideCopy()`) is a lost log, not a new phone. A phone with neither is new. Checks: damaged log -> restart (silent, `coversFrom = writtenAt`, flag saved) -> restart (still silent) -> network time (history start set, flag cleared, kept after a restart), the same below 13 (start at once, never now minus 90 days, also saved by the constructor), a deleted log beside a copy on 13+ and below, a new phone.
  **S1 done.** Only a session that STARTED unchecked is marked; one that started checked and ended in a hold is sent (its pieces are all trusted). Check: a receiver keeps its pieces (the file carries the session).
  **S2 done.** The file is stamped with the session clock only while a real session runs (`PhoneSessionSyncWriter.forFile`); otherwise with the phone's trusted time. **N4:** `syncInput()` upgrades an unchecked clock when no session runs, so the file stops being silent at once when network time arrives.
  **S3 done.** The heartbeat receiver (`PhoneSessionAlarmReceiver`) keeps `goAsync` open until the folder, Drive and relay passes are done (`CompanionApp.sendOwnFileAndWait`, 8 s at most). The heartbeat alarm is set 5 minutes after the LAST ask (never under 1 minute from now), so a refresh that wrote nothing does not push it back (PS2a's pin changed: 3 minutes after a refresh 2 minutes in). Q1180 stays at 5 minutes.
  **S4 done.** `syncFocusReceived` is built with `flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)` and starts from the received list; the controller lock is held only for the snapshot (the 90-day cutting is outside it). **S5 done.** `SyncFileStrip.forQr(file, zone)` keeps today's sessions only (the open one ends at the file's time) and raises `coversFrom` to the newest end left out, and trims the drift as the PC does; the PC's further trimming to the codes' room is NOT ported.
  **Q1181 done (the reviewer's fix).** Each unchecked ended record keeps the clock anchor its times were written on, saved with its mark (`PhoneSessionUncheckedEnd`). In the same boot the network time corrects each one by its own anchor, also after the process died. A record or a running start from an earlier boot (the checkpoint now saves `startBoot`) stays unchecked and out of the file, and is never shifted by a later boot's correction.   **V-S1 done.** The heartbeat receiver's wait is now `PhoneSessionBoundedWait.run(timeoutMs)` (new, pure JVM): the passes run on their own thread and the wait is a timed `join`, so a pass blocked on a socket or file can't hold the receiver past 8 s (`pending.finish()` stays in `finally`); the passes go on afterwards. I used a thread, not `appScope.launch` + `withTimeoutOrNull(job.join())`, because the checks module has no coroutines library (no new dependency) and the bound must be tested with a really blocking pass: check "a pass that blocks 3 s is waited for 0.3 s" (also: a quick pass is waited for to its end; a throwing pass does not throw into the receiver). Mutations: scope back, join without timeout.

Mutations: overwrite anyway, saves not locked.

(`AccountabiliBuddy.App.csproj` is the earlier local version bump, not mine.)

**How it works.**
- `DeviceRolesService` (document `sync.deviceRoles`, device-local): `Held` = merged list written into this PC's file at once; `Applied` = what the readers use; `Pending` = a change waiting. Change detected by `Effective(applied)` vs `Effective(held)` (`PhonesChanged` + label differences), never by entries or stamps; a new target restarts the wait, the same target seen again does not.
- Gate (every change this PC asks for, both modes): `SyncDeviceRoleGate` over this PC's own send log (`SyncSendLog.NewestPutByMethod`) and the CONFIGURED methods (`SyncCoordinator.OwnMethods`). Delay (10 min, `Locks.LooseningDelay`) only for a change of "the phones", AB mode, counted from the gate opening (a received or derived change: from first sight), never while a session runs (own or seen elsewhere), no "apply now". PB: applies at once between sessions once the gate is open. Cancel = `SyncDeviceRoles.Restore(held, applied)`, only for own requests, never refused.
- Status line on the device's row: "Waiting to reach Google Drive..." > "On Google Drive · applies at 4:15 PM" > "Applied" (5 min). Role in the row subtitle ("main phone", "tablet -> main phone"), icon by role, "Role" submenu + Cancel in the row's menu, one status line under the subtitle (no new column).
- Readers via `ThePhones(received)`: wake alarm, wake estimate, smart bedtime, daily steps (`FollowPhoneAlarm`), NG1 `GroupHasPhone`, both `HasLinkedPhone`, `StatsViewModel` bedtime. Unchanged on purpose (by kind): sessions, placement, pickups, work-ended, QR, profiles, removal. A scan check proves no file outside sync/Devices/assistant/readers mentions a role.
- Receipts: roles count with the `settings` part. Leaving the group forgets the roles.
- SetupFeatures `devices.roles` ("0.1.137"): relevant from 2 Android devices, set up when each has a role in the merged list; page `AssistantStep.DeviceRoles` (`DeviceRolesPageView`). Not in the full setup.

**Hand tests (owner, test group with 2+ Android devices).**
1. Devices & sync: phone's menu > Role > Main phone. Row shows "Waiting to reach Google Drive..." then "On Google Drive · applies at <time>", then the subtitle says "main phone". Cancel while waiting puts everything back.
2. Start a focus session: the Role items are disabled with the reason; a change due meanwhile says "applies when your session ends".
3. The PC's alarm / bedtime / wake estimate keep the old phone's until the wait ends, then follow the main phone only.
4. Turn Drive off while waiting: the status moves to the shared folder or relay; a broken Drive sign-in stays "Waiting".
5. Settings > What's new shows "Android devices: which is your main phone" only with 2+ Android devices; picks go through the same wait.
6. ProductaBuddy: a change applies right after the upload, between sessions.
Questions Q1188-Q1192.

**Steps tiles and row (B10 table, both modes).**
- One steps rule per goal. The two tiles are two ways of counting it: no steps source = both say "Add" (Health Connect makes the rule with All, Pixel Watch with Watch); with one, the tile that matches is "Selected" (Health Connect for All and Phone, Pixel Watch for Watch), the other says "Use this" and switches the existing rule (never a second one). A tap on a selected tile does nothing. A steps tile never says "Added".
- The added row has an All | Watch | Phone control (the only way to pick Phone, as the owner asked for two tiles) and its title follows the table: "Health Connect · steps" / "Pixel Watch · steps (your phone's on days without watch steps)" / "This phone · steps". The Detail line says what each counts and names the main phone ("Counted on <name>, your main phone") when roles in use here have one (`GoalEditorLookups.MainPhoneName`).
- Saving writes `StepsFrom` on the rule through `ToRule`; every switch is a Sources change, so the store stamps the part once at save. An untouched row saves the same rule. `GoalDetailViewModel` source line says "Daily steps from your watch (your phone's on days without)" / "...from your phone itself" for Watch / Phone. The day panel shows the entry's class word ("from your watch", "from your phone (no watch steps that day)", "from your phone"), and "from Health Connect" while no entry carries a word (`GoalDetailViewModel.StepsWordOf`).
- Switching recounts every covered day: checked through Core `GoalCounting` over the editor's saved goal (all / Watch / Phone give three different day lists; the synced total stops counting for Watch and Phone).

**ST4 in the editor.**
- Each entry has a "Devices" chip (inline, see Q1202): "All ticked devices", or the chosen ones ("SA, Laptop"), "No device". Boxes: an "All ticked devices" box plus one per device ticked on the source. All boxes ticked = no list (null). A list is ids only. Ids of devices that left or were unticked on the source stay in the entry's list. Untouched entries are saved as the same object (unknown fields kept); a changed device list keeps the entry's unknown fields. A changed kind or text is a new entry (B7: no unknown fields) that keeps the chosen devices.
- "Counts on none of the ticked devices" warning (Core `CountsNowhere`), not a save block. Under the Counts from row, one line per ticked device (2 or more ticked) lists what counts there, or "Nothing counts here".
- Save runs Core `OneEntryPerKindAndText`: a duplicate (e.g. after a kind-chip change) joins device lists, a twin with no list makes the entry count everywhere (B7).
- Pick lists: "Seen on this PC" (this PC's 30 days, as before) and, beside it, "From your goals" (entries already in any live goal's screen-time rules, deduped, device lists dropped, minus what this source lists and what the seen list offers). Any app id or site can be typed (a package id becomes a Phone app) and given to the phone only. No new synced list; nothing new leaves the device.
- PC counting: `GoalScreenTimeSource.CountedRules` / `ReadingsFor` use `CountsOn(rule, match, device)` with this PC's id, so an entry that names only other devices is not read here, and a rule with no entry for this PC is not counted (no days needed, nothing written).
- Q1201 (stale entries of a device whose entries were all excluded kept counting) was fixed in Core on the coordinator's order: `GoalCounting.CountsOnDevice` (see below).

**Hand tests (use a test goal you create, delete it after).**
1. Goals > New goal (Amount, steps) > Tracking & sources: with a phone in the group the Health Connect and Pixel Watch tiles both say "Add". Add Pixel Watch: the row says "Pixel Watch · steps (...)", Watch is lit in the control, the Pixel Watch tile says "Selected" and Health Connect says "Use this". Click Health Connect: row says "Health Connect · steps", All lit. Click Phone in the control: "This phone · steps", Health Connect "Selected". Save, reopen: it is still Phone. Delete the goal.
2. New Time goal > add Screen time (sync on, 2+ other devices): tick the phone and the laptop under Counts from; add `youtube.com`, `chrome.exe`, then type `com.example.chat`. Chip on com.example.chat > untick "All ticked devices" and tick only the phone; each device's line under Counts from lists what counts there. Untick every box on chrome.exe: "Counts on none of the ticked devices" shows. Save and reopen: the lists are kept. Delete the goal.
3. Open a second goal's screen-time source: a package id you added to the first goal shows under "From your goals"; a tap adds it for all ticked devices. Delete both goals.
Questions Q1201-Q1205.

**Q1201 Core fix (`src/AccountabiliBuddy.Core/Goals/GoalCounting.cs` only).** `Combined` (ScreenTime branch) kept a device's stored entries when `CountsOn(rule, device)` held. It now uses `CountsOnDevice(rule, device)` = `CountsOn(rule, device)` AND (the rule has no entry list OR some entry has `CountsOn(rule, match, device)`). Checks: 9 `sr1: st4/counting:` cases (Core 2763/2763 = 2754 + 9). Mutation M52 (`sr1s1_mutations.py`) fails 5 of them, file restored byte for byte. The fixture is regenerated by every Core run (`Sr1Fixtures.WriteAll`).

### PS2c done (phone session lock seam and enforcement; `android/` only, not committed)
`assembleDebug :checks:run --offline --no-daemon`: green, 5126/5126 (91 new `ps2c`). 32 mutations (`scratchpad/ps2c_mutations.py`, out of repo), each fails at least 1 check (most 2-6), each restored byte for byte (sha256 asserted): lock door ignoring the own scope, PB scope with Plan, AB scope without removal, any-running without own, pause gate / guidance gate dropped, own end cost free, stricter-cost free, blocks (end turns off ones that were on before, already-on become ours, refused turn-on kept as ours, ignoring emergency, never asked), usage (pickups outside focus, no access as zero, overwrite, test session read, focus ignoring away, shortest stretch), removal lock without SYNC, guard switch / calendar overlay / plan lock / a placement consumer on the wrong door, own record compared with a wall clock, profile ignored by the hook, later end = earlier, profiles changeable in a session, PB LMP rows unlocked, GuardsScope ignoring the area, `locked()` ignoring another device.

**Design.** `PlacementSession` has three doors: `current` (PC, else test; the PLACEMENT door, never the own session; 7 placement-only consumers keep it), `locking(app, now, area)` (every lock consumer: PC/test lock all areas, own locks its scope by mode through `PhoneSessionLocks`, both = stricter; the own session is the store's flag, no clock, B1) and `anyRunning` (S4/S8). `GuardsLock.at(app, now, area = OTHER_SETTINGS)` is the old full lock (so a PB phone session leaves plain Settings open) plus `areasAt` for the area rows. Area map: removal lock = REMOVAL + SYNC (the SYNC seam for SR1's role refusal: call `PlacementSession.locking(app, now, PhoneLockArea.SYNC)`), plan sentence and `EventActions` = PLAN, `PlacementGuard` = GUARDS_OTHER, sleep sync / calendar overlay / Settings pages = OTHER_SETTINGS. The written classification of all 16 consumer files is in `Ps2cSelfCheck` (a scan fails on any unclassified use). Compose: `LocalGuardsAreas` (from `CompanionViewModel.guardsAreas`), `GuardsScope(area)` on the Essentials/LMP and Curbox sections.
- **S8/S4 (MUSTs, done):** `pause.sessionRunning` and `guidance.sessionRunning` = `PlacementSession.anyRunning` (a pending ProductaBuddy and a pause request wait while the phone's own session runs; the paid "now" still applies). `SessionEndCost.now` merges the own cost (`SessionEndCostRules.own`: AB timed = not free, the phone's wait/reason; PB and no-set-end free) with a PC's (`stricter`). Q1207, Q1208.
- **Profiles:** `shared/session/PhoneSessionProfile.kt` (+ JSON), `app/session/PhoneSessionProfiles.kt` (prefs `phoneSessionProfiles`; `replaceAll` refuses in a real session; `enforcementOf(record)` by the record's profile id, N6). `EssentialsLaunch.ownProfile` now reads it (was `NONE`). No editor yet.
- **Curbox blocks:** `CurboxSessionBlocksRule` (pure) + `curbox/CurboxSessionBlocks.kt`, called first in `CurboxHardMode.check`; only blocks that were off are ours and turned off at the end; emergency lifts them (Q1209). The profile's group already flows through `EssentialsLaunch.curboxGroup`.
- **Pickups/distraction:** `PhoneSessionUsageRules` (pure), `PhoneSessionUsage.read(app, record, end)` (blocking; needs Usage access, else nulls shown as a dash), `PhoneSessionRecord.usage` (codec), `controller.recordUsage` (set once), saved off-thread by the runtime at every real session end. Never in the sync file (scan).
- No direct `sendSessionLock` / `LMP_STOP` path. `essentials/` untouched this task. Gradle: `checks` now also compiles `GuidanceRules.kt` and `PauseRules.kt`.

**For PS3a.**
- Start may now be exposed (S4/S8 gates are in). Live card line: `PhoneSessionUsage.read(app, record, app.phoneSession.controller.now())` off the main thread; ended sessions: `controller.log.last().usage` (null parts = dash).
- Profiles for the Start sheet: `app.phoneSessionProfiles.all()`; editing is PS3b (`replaceAll`; the pages must also show the lock banner for areas SESSION_PROFILES and END_EARLY_SETTING, via `LocalGuardsAreas`).
- The pause section's "resting" text does not cover a PB phone session (Q1207).
- The End sheet's LMP line (Q1193) and the End cost in the mode switch read `SessionEndCostRules`/`PhoneEndSettings` through `app.phoneSession.endSettings()`.
- PB phone session with a profile that has LMP/Curbox: those rows lock through `GuardsScope(area)` only in sections already wrapped (Essentials, Curbox); a new section for them must pass its area.
