### PS3a done (the phone's Today session UI: Start, the running session, End, "Running on <device>"; `android/` only, not committed)
`assembleDebug :checks:run --offline --no-daemon`: green, 5235/5235 (109 more than PS2c's 5126; 109 are `ps3a`; one `ps2c` pin was reworded on purpose, see below). 45 mutations (`scratchpad/ps3a_mutations.py`, out of repo), each fails at least 1 check, each restored byte for byte (sha256 asserted): target (remembered phone ignored, offline PC as default, online ignoring a live PC session / two more quiet hours), blocks (live elsewhere, waiting request, "paused"), End sheet (Confirm without reason, End session without reason, End before the wait, PB asking the wait, All tasks done before 10 minutes, a stale End keeping its wait, no LockMyPhone line), snapshot (engine / controller / codec / starter drop the copy, the hook / Curbox blocks / usage apps read the live store), key (phase start, hook keyed by phase), All tasks done (ticked tasks leave the list, Queued at start, unloaded list read as "all removed", Undo not telling the session, bridge never started, tick of the wrong id), Start (opens while blocked, bar while blocked, goal menu skipping the block, PC panel on while the PC is offline, remembered target ignored), End wiring (closing keeps an unconfirmed End, wait never opens, End now as on-command, a 24-hour time), clock (starter on the wall clock, card tick on the phone's clock), texts (old mode-switch words), armed end not listed, SetupFeatures (wrong version, page missing), menu (End session ends at once). One run read SURVIVED once (mutation "PB asks the wait") and was a gradle hiccup: the script now says NO-RESULT when no totals line was printed, and the re-run killed it (5 checks).

**Every PS3a must-do (`ps3-mustdos.md`).**
- **Profile snapshot (S3):** Start copies `PhoneSessionProfile.snapshot()` (LockMyPhone profile, Curbox group and blocks, distracting apps) into `PhoneSessionRecord.snapshot` (codec, sealed store, never the sync file; `toString` hides the app list). `PhoneSessionProfiles.enforcementOf`, `CurboxSessionBlocks` and `PhoneSessionUsage` read the record, never the live store; a scan pins that the profiles' prefs are named in one file. A record with no copy (none exists in practice) turns nothing on.
- **Own-session key (S4 / Q1199):** done here. `OwnSessionEnforcement.keyOf(record) = startedAt`; `EssentialsLaunch.ownSessionStart` uses it, so Resume, lunch end and the phone-off gap no longer restart Curbox's group, the hold or the lunch lift's re-lock match.
- **End sheet LMP line (Q1193):** `EssentialsLaunch.lockOutlivesSession` (LMP installed and verified, no hold, `KEY_SESSION_LOCK_FOR` is this session's key) gives the session's end; the sheet says "LockMyPhone-Extended keeps its lock until 4:05 PM." and offers "Update LockMyPhone-Extended" (the ADR 74 installer: download, then the install page).
- **All tasks done (Q1171, B6):** `PhoneSessionTasks` (started in `PhoneSessionRuntime.start`) follows the task lists while a real session runs and hands `SessionTasksFromModel.whileRunning` to `controller.thisSessionChanged`; every toast Undo in `ShelfController.undoOf` calls `tasks.undone()` (`thisSessionUndone`). Open tasks come in the model's order, finished ones after by tick time; a task moved out open is a drop; an unloaded list (`!taskModel.isActive`) never counts as "all removed". The list at Start is This session's open tasks.
- **Mode-switch dialog (N4):** `PhoneSessionTexts.modeSwitchLocksLine(phoneSession)`; a phone session says what "now" changes (only the profile's locks, End free).
- **Test session (N5):** unchanged on purpose (still starts during an own session; harmless, own wins in the hook order).
- **Also done (review asks):** the pause section's "turn off" request now reads as resting during a PB phone session (Q1207's line); `RunningWaits` lists the own armed end ("This phone's session ends in 0:42", Cancel is free) on Today and in the pinned header's Cancel.

**What was built (no boards for Start sheet, Running-on bar, End sheet: R2b sheet patterns, `AbSheet`, `AbSegmented`, chips, said in each file).**
- **Start** (`ui/today/StartSheet.kt`, `StartSessionSheetHost`): the thumb zone's bar is "Start" and opens a Tall sheet. Target: "On <PC>" / "On this phone" (`AbSegmented`), only "On this phone" with no PC (R15). `PhoneStartRules.defaultTarget` (remembered wins when available, else the controlled PC when online, else this phone; "<PC> isn't online (last heard 2:05 PM)" or the update line under the PC option); each choice is remembered in prefs `phoneSession` `target`. On this phone: profile chips (built-in "Focus" = a plain timer, then the saved profiles; `PhoneSessionProfiles.choices()`), length 25 / 50 / 90 / Custom (1 to 720) / No set end, a one-line mode note, Start. On <PC>: the ADR 42 start as before (profile, 25 / 50 / 90, Quick Start, No set end), off while the PC is not online. R3's "<name>'s session last heard from 2:05 PM" is the sheet's subtitle and a muted line on Today.
- **Blocks (R1, R2, R3, B15):** `rememberGroupLive` (trusted time, every 15 s) → `PhoneStartRules.block`: own session > live elsewhere ("Running on Pixel · 32 min left", "· paused", "· no set end") > a waiting start request ("Waiting for <PC>'s answer", the existing Stop waiting line) > ADR 60's settings rest. A block replaces the Start bar with the non-pressable `RunningElsewhereBar` (same height, nothing moves) and closes an open sheet; the controller still refuses a start over a live session.
- **The running session** (`OwnSessionView`): the record becomes the PC-shaped `PcState` / `TodayPc` / `RunningTasks` the existing cards read, so the session card (ring, "left · ends 1:23 PM", paused hold), This session, the action bar (Done, Step away / I'm back, ⋯) and the Today header are the PC's. Ticks go through the shared task model (`ShelfController.finish` / `tickStep`). The card's seconds tick on the session clock (`displayNow`), so moving the phone's clock changes nothing.
- **Session menu** (`OwnSessionMenuSheet`): Step away / I'm back, Pause timer / Resume, Add 10 minutes (live exactly when `PhoneSessionEngine.add10` would do it, else the engine's reason), "End and mark the rest unfinished" greyed ("Not for a session on this phone"), End session → the End sheet.
- **End** (`EndPhoneSessionSheet`, pure `PhoneEndSheet`): AB timed: the wait counts from opening (`openEnd`), a ring, the reason field with a character count, "Confirm · ends in 0:42" (arms the end, applies at the wait's end by the alarm, even with the screen locked), "End session" after the wait, "Keep going" (free); an armed end shows "ends by itself at 12:06 PM" with "Cancel the end"; closing an unconfirmed End drops it. PB and a session with no set end: "End now". "All tasks done · End now" when R9 holds. A parity check runs every second of the wait against the engine: the sheet's button ends exactly when the engine ends.
- **Goals:** the goal menu's "Start a session…" opens the same sheet under the same block; `NO_PC_TEXT`, the preset page and "Phone sessions are coming." are gone. `TodayPane`'s parameters are now `pcFiles` and `fileActions` (the own session takes their place); `MainScreen` passes them.
- **SetupFeatures:** `PHONE_SESSIONS = "session.onPhone"`, since 0.1.80, `PhoneSessionSetupPage` (explains; nothing asked), set up once seen, a profile exists or a phone session was ever started.

**Deliberate change to an earlier check:** `ps2c/sources (N6)` pinned `byId(record.profileId)` in `enforcementOf`; S3 replaces that with the record's copy, so the pin now says `record.snapshot.enforcement` and no `byId`.

**Not done / for others.** The pinned notification's own-session countdown (HeaderSource builds from other devices' files; PS5's `PhoneSnapshot` has the PC-only filter): only the armed-end wait and Cancel are wired (Q1216). The profile editor, the end-early setting and Settings rows are PS3b; until then Start offers only the built-in "Focus" (Q1217). Tablet layout is the PC-shaped adapter (header "Focus on this phone"), no polish. `StartOnPcDialog` (PcCard.kt) is no longer used; not deleted (Q1218).

**Files (stage the new ones with `git add`; all under `android/`).** New: `app/.../companion/session/{PhoneSessionStarter,PhoneSessionTasks}.kt`, `app/.../companion/ui/PhoneSessionSetupPage.kt`, `app/.../companion/ui/today/{EndPhoneSessionSheet,GroupLive,OwnSessionMenuSheet,OwnSessionView,StartSheet}.kt`, `shared/.../shared/session/{PhoneEndSheet,PhoneSessionProfileSnapshot,PhoneSessionTexts,PhoneStartRules,SessionTasksFromModel}.kt`, `checks/.../checks/Ps3aSelfCheck.kt`. Modified: `app/.../companion/{curbox/CurboxSessionBlocks,essentials/EssentialsLaunch,notify/RunningWaits,setup/SetupFeatures}.kt`, `.../session/{PhoneSessionController,PhoneSessionProfiles,PhoneSessionRuntime,PhoneSessionUsage}.kt`, `.../ui/{GuidanceSection,MainScreen,PauseSection,PcStepAwayRow,PhoneToday,TodayCards,TodayHero,TodayModels,TodayPane,WhatsNewScreen}.kt`, `.../ui/goals/{GoalMenuSheet,GoalSessionStart}.kt`, `.../ui/tasks/page/ShelfController.kt`, `.../ui/today/{SessionMenuSheet,ThisSessionCard,TodayActionBar}.kt`; `shared/.../shared/session/{OwnSessionEnforcement,PhoneSessionCodec,PhoneSessionEngine,PhoneSessionProfile,PhoneSessionRecord}.kt`; `checks/.../checks/{Main,Ps2cSelfCheck}.kt`. No wait loop, monitor or background process of mine is left running.

**Hand tests for the orchestrator (device; make your own test sessions and tasks, end them and delete the tasks after).**
1. No group (or the PC app closed 16 minutes): Start opens on "On this phone" with only that option, or with the PC note "<PC> isn't online (last heard …)". Start a 2-minute Custom session with "Focus": card, timer, This session with two tasks you made.
2. PC online: Start opens on the PC; pick "On this phone", close, reopen: the phone is remembered. Pick the PC again, start it (Quick Start), end it on the PC.
3. During your phone session the PC's title bar says it is running on the phone; the PC's Start refuses (PS4).
4. Step away / I'm back (text "Away since …"), Pause timer then Resume (the end moves by the pause), Add 10 minutes (end moves; refused while paused / no set end). Start a lunch on the PC: the countdown holds.
5. AB, a timed session: ⋯ → End session → the wait ring and the reason; type 15+ characters, "Confirm · ends in 0:xx", lock the screen: it ends at the wait's end. Open End and tap "Keep going": nothing is kept. Confirm, then "Cancel the end" (also from the Today wait line and the pinned header). PB: "End now". A no-set-end session: "End now" in AB too.
6. Tick every task of a session started with tasks, wait 10 minutes: "All tasks done · End now" ends it at once. Move one open task to Queued first: it never offers it (Undo from the toast brings it back).
7. With another phone (or a second profile on it) running a session: "Running on <name> · N min left", Start off; stop its sync for 16 minutes: Start comes back with "<name>'s session last heard from …".
8. Goals tab → a goal's menu → Start a session…: the same sheet.
9. AB phone session, Settings › Guidance: switch to ProductaBuddy "during this session": the dialog's line says what "now" changes. Settings › Pause: a "turn off" request waits "until your session ends" in PB.
10. Phone clock +1 hour in a session: the countdown, the End wait and the armed end do not move. With an older LockMyPhone-Extended (no hold) and a lock asked for: the End sheet line and "Update LockMyPhone-Extended".
11. Kill the app mid-session and reopen: it carries on; the End sheet opened before the kill starts its wait again after 10 minutes.
